- **quantile-deviation-visualization-across-structure-types**: Use when you have per-base translocation times from Tombo re-squiggled ONT reads and motif coordinates (e.g. non-B DNA DB hg38 annotations), and need to test which non-B structure classes show significant TT quantile deviations from B-DNA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/quantile-deviation-visualization-across-structure-types))
- **quantile-distributional-methods**: Estimate conditional or unconditional distributional effects with quantile regression, quantile forests, or RIF regression. ([ShijunLei-cn/AI-Economist-Skills](https://github.com/ShijunLei-cn/AI-Economist-Skills/tree/HEAD/econometrics_in_python/quantile-distributional-methods))
- **quantile-normalization-ablation-comparison**: Use when building or validating a Ciclops cross-platform transfer model on transcriptomic datasets (e.g., RNA-seq train, microarray test) and you need to decide whether quantile normalization should be kept on or skipped via --no_quantile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/quantile-normalization-ablation-comparison))
- **quantile-normalization-application**: Use when after autoQ has extracted peak area measurements for isotopologues from GC-CI-MS data and you need to prepare these integrations for barplot visualization with metBarPlot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/quantile-normalization-application))
- **quantile-normalization-cross-platform**: Use when preparing .csv training and validation expression matrices with unequal platform distributions and missing values before running Ciclops cross-platform clinical outcome prediction, or when reproducing the Ciclops preprocessing stage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/quantile-normalization-cross-platform))
- **quantile-normalization-of-compartment-scores**: Use when compartment scores (PC1/PC2 vectors) have been computed for multiple Hi-C datasets or replicates and must be made comparable across conditions prior to differential compartment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/quantile-normalization-of-compartment-scores))
